# Changelog

All notable changes to CandleViewer are documented here. Format follows
`docs/plan/07-release-and-prr.md` §3: entries are grouped by category under
each release heading, generated from Conventional Commits by
`tools/ci/update_changelog.py` in reviewed sync PRs (previewed on every merge
by `.github/workflows/changelog.yml`, which never commits) and finalised at
release-train cut time by `.github/workflows/release.yml`. Security-relevant entries describe impact
only, never exploit detail (full detail is kept in the internal finding
tracker).

## Unreleased

### Features

- **infra-devops**: E03-T11 add release workflow, changelog and semver tagging (#1565) (15bbbd8)
- **infra-devops**: wire coverage/bundle-size/flaky quality gates to §9 (#1543) (605cb2f)
- **infra-devops**: E03-T08 build, scan, SBOM and cosign-sign images on merge to main (#1540) (4dc6bb7)
- **backend-platform**: bootstrap Postgres relational tier (E07-T02) (#1551) (a04a3b4)
- **backend-platform**: E50-T57 pin xstate-statemachine==0.9.1 with hash lock + CI attestation (#1553) (f6e7530)
- **infra-devops**: enforce per-package coverage thresholds as a blocking CI gate (#1519) (8a4abc7)
- **infra-devops**: wire contract, integration, e2e and a11y CI lanes (#1521) (3254655)
- **infra-devops**: E03-T03 Python CI lane with ruff, mypy strict, pytest and uv caching (#1501) (49995ca)
- **chart-engine**: E06-K02 spike -- scene A 100k-bar model, visible-window draw, LOD ladder (#1509) (adb4c23)
- **chart-engine**: E06-K01 M0 benchmark harness and deterministic seeded fixture generator (#1503) (472083d)
- **infra-devops**: E03-T07 SAST/SCA/secrets/licence/container scanning lane (#1502) (77fb5ab)
- **infra-devops**: E03-T05 generated-code freshness gate for packages/protocol (#1499) (e3ecce3)
- **infra-devops**: E03-T01 Actions PR skeleton with path filters and checks resolver (#1488) (420d499)
- **ingestion**: E08-K01 spike -- book depth-tier and cadence policy (#1493) (a6abcf7)
- **ingestion**: E08-T03 in-process topic bus with bounded queues and backpressure policy (#1492) (8d912d5)
- **ingestion**: E08-T01 ExchangeAdapter ports, capability record, error taxonomy (#1491) (72075a7)
- **backend-platform**: E07-T01 M10 storage package repositories + import-linter driver gate (#1490) (ad4149c)
- **backend-platform**: E02-T12 synthetic-feed dev loop + onboarding docs (#1489) (162e5d9)
- **backend-platform**: E02-T09 protocol codegen from OpenAPI + WS schema (#1486) (0bf7ec9)
- **auth-rbac**: add identity/RBAC/session/MFA migration (E09-T01) (#1485) (8489633)
- **frontend-platform**: scaffold apps/web and apps/desktop skeletons (#1481) (252c562)
- **backend-platform**: scaffold services/api module tree M1-M24, composition root and typed settings (#1472) (1035bc2)
- **auth-rbac**: Tailscale-only reachability self-check and CIDR guard (#1457) (d5c6c61)
- **docs**: implement board automation kind-sync and QA/security/a11y close guards (#1436) (5abade5)
- **design-system**: E05-D01 token foundations for 3 themes x 2 densities (#1437) (86353aa)
- **docs**: refresh PR template and issue forms to the ticket taxonomy (#1429) (50add18)
- **docs**: publish backlog ticket JSON schema and GOV-004 validator (#1431) (a2d2f5c)
- **docs**: add GOV-001 CODEOWNERS coverage checker (#1430) (ebffa41)
